科技動能支撐股市，利率風險牽動債市配置
2026 年 9 月
全球股市 8 月截至 26 日多數上漲，其中成熟市場上漲 3.1%居冠，新興亞洲、東協股市與標普
500 也分別上漲 2.9%、2.7%及 2.6%。美股受企業財報韌性與月初就業、通膨數據降溫支撐；亞洲
則受美元偏弱、油價與長端殖利率回落帶動風險偏好改善。然而，中國股市期間下跌 0.3%，該國 7
月零售、工業生產與投資數據偏弱，加上阿里巴巴折價配股一度衝擊科技股，惟公司高層後續增持有
助緩解信心壓力 。 拉美股市下跌0.8%，在主要股市中敬陪末座 ， 主要拖累來自巴西 ；8 月截至 24 日，

外資淨流出巴西股市約 214 億雷亞爾，加上財政與 10 月大選的不確定性壓抑市場。印度亦小跌
0.3%，因高油價與 RBI 偏鷹訊號使公債殖利率上升。
全球債市 8 月截至 26 日則全面收紅 ， 以新興當地債上漲1.53%居冠 ， 美元指數回落至99 附近，
為其帶來匯兌加成；亞洲高收益債與全球高收益債分別上漲 1.16%、1.09%，高票息與較短存續期提
供支撐 。 亞洲債券 、 全球主權債與新興美元債表現相對落後 。8 月中美國 30 年期殖利率一度升至 2007
年以來高點，壓抑長存續期債券；其後油價回落，加上8/19 財政部宣布 9/9 起將 10 至 30 年期公債
買回上限至少倍增至每次 40 億美元，長端流動性疑慮獲緩解，30 年期殖利率回落，因而帶動債券價
格修復。
展望 9 月，市場焦點將集中於 9 月 15 日至 16 日的 FOMC 會議，以及最新經濟預測與點陣圖；
全球股市預料呈現高檔震盪格局。儘管物價漲勢稍有緩和，但 Fed 對通膨仍保持警戒；若政策立場
較市場預期鷹派，可能推升美債殖利率波動，並壓抑高估值成長股走勢。不過，AI 基礎建設與企業

科技投資趨勢延續，美國企業獲利成長亦有利科技股表現，預料中期基本面仍具支撐。台灣受 AI 與
半導體出口需求帶動，經濟成長動能強勁；日股則持續受企業治理改革與資本效率改善支撐。整體而
言，9 月股市短線震盪難免，但中期仍看好科技股，以及基本面相對強勁的台灣與日本股市。
就債市而言，美債殖利率預料仍將處於相對高檔；若就業持續降溫且通膨未再明顯升高，中期利
率上行空間可望受限。期限配置方面，短端對 Fed 政策預期較敏感，過短的存續期間亦伴隨再投資
風險；10 年以上債券則持續承受財政與期限溢酬壓力。相較之下，3 至 10 年期債券兼具較高息收、
適度存續期間與參與利率下行的空間。整體而言，目前 3 至 10 年期美國公債及投資級債的風險報酬
條件相對均衡，較具表現機會。

美國股市：AI 熱潮推升企業獲利，美股續強但留意利率風險
八月美股在高檔震盪中收高，科技股表現亮眼，除整體美股優異財報支撐指數表現外，AI 龍頭企
業如 NVIDIA 均給出強勁展望 ，AI 行情由晶片硬體向軟體與資安應用端擴散 。指數上揚主要由輝達、
微軟、亞馬遜、帕蘭提爾、特斯拉、Meta、美光科技、賽富時、Marvell、默克藥廠等企業帶動，表
現相對弱勢的族群包括蘋果、博通、沃爾瑪、TJX、AppLovin、威騰、聯合健康、CVS、應用材料、
超微半導體等。
聯準會新任主席試圖以新的溝通模式取代傳統前瞻指引，市場解讀為基準利率有上行空間且更長

時間維持高檔。聯準會主席華許在全球央行年會(Jackson Hole)拒絕提供傳統的前瞻指引或機械式的
反應函數，強調央行過度承諾未來利率路徑會失去政策彈性，以一套「決策準則」取代指引，重點觀
察潛在通膨是否快速回到 2%、勞動市場是否偏離充分就業，以及金融條件是否有效限制總需求。由
於 7 月 FOMC 會議出現官員主張升息的情況，使短端與長端美債殖利率走勢分化，殖利率曲線進一
步陡峭化 ， 市場正在質疑 Fed 是否具備一致且可理解的決策框架 ， 但在華許發言後 ， 按照這套標準，
目前政策訊號仍偏向利率在較高水準維持更長時間。由於通膨問題仍具廣度，同時信用利差偏低、股
市高檔、AI 投資與私人需求持續強勁，很難將目前金融條件視為具有限制性，故市場預期 聯準會可
能不只提高升息機率，也提高了升息後維持高利率的可能性，甚至進一步提高未來降息的門檻。
第二季財報公布大致落幕，表現優於先前預期。目前標普 500 成分股已有 459 家公布 2Q26 財
報，其中 86%獲利優於市場預期，並帶動 2Q26 獲利年增達約 31.6%，較季初市場預估的 21%大幅

上修；全年獲利年增亦由 23%上修至 27%。類股獲利年增動能以能源 (YoY+145%)、資訊科技
(YoY+71%)、原材料(YoY+41%)增長動能最強，醫療保健類股(YoY-7%)表現落後。資訊科技類股獲
利成長主要由半導體族群帶動，次產業中半導體獲利年增 139%、科技硬體與設備獲利年增 50.9%。
美國期中選舉前的「資料中心用電排擠效應引發的民粹反彈」持續發酵。隨著 AI 資料中心龐大
的電力需求，引發地方民意對民生與傳統產業用電資源遭擠壓的疑慮持續升溫 。 各州政府在選舉壓力
下，部分地方政府緊急凍結資料中心計畫，預料 11 月期中選舉後政治干擾將可降溫。
目前標普 500 指數位於歷史高檔位置，本益比已升至 23.51 倍、股價淨值比達 5.6 倍，短期評價
偏高。雖然美國科技業者目前仍居 AI 產業領先地位，但股市漲幅已高，短期仍需留意市場過熱與估
值偏高風險，建議以長線角度分批布局。

歐洲股市：景氣溫和復甦，歐洲央行升息預期與政治變數限制評價 
八月歐股延續多頭走勢，泛歐600 指數中原物料與科技股表現相對強勢，包括思愛普、艾司摩爾
控股、Argenx、施耐德電機、力拓、嘉能可、英美資源、安聯集團、勞斯萊斯控股、西門子等表現
較佳；相較之下，部分金融與消費股表現偏弱，包括雀巢、匯豐控股、諾和諾德、英美菸草、阿斯特
捷利康、法國巴黎銀行、法國興業銀行、諾華、百威英博、萬喜等。 
歐元區八月綜合 PMI 升至 52.1，連續第 2 個月站上榮枯線，顯示第 3 季初景氣延續擴張。其中
製造業升至 52.8，創 2022 年 5 月以來最佳表現，復甦已由生產端擴散至需求端。AI 相關科技與設
備需求增加，加上國防支出擴大，帶動製造業需求回升；服務業則持平於 51.7，受惠於旅遊維持溫
和擴張。國家別來看，德國製造業 PMI 升至 54.1，創 2022 年以來最佳表現，法國製造業重返擴張
區間，但極端高溫拖累服務業活動。整體而言，八月 PMI 顯示歐元區景氣動能明顯改善，製造業加
速復甦，短期衰退風險進一步下降，然而服務業擴張力道未見加快，復甦步調呈現分化。 
部分歐洲央行官員表示，九月已有充分理由升息，利率交換市場目前反映 ECB 在 9 月會議升息
一碼的可能性。美伊戰爭推高能源成本，加上歐洲經濟表現穩健，使市場認為 ECB 仍有收緊貨幣政
策的空間。市場焦點因此由「ECB 會不會升息」轉向「升息循環還會延續多久」 。中東局勢仍是最大
變數，若能源成本持續走高且逐步傳導至商品、服務價格與薪資，ECB 第四季仍可能維持鷹派立場。 
9 月歐股須留意政治局勢。若 AfD（德國另類選擇黨）於地方選舉取得大勝，雖不會直接改變目
前政府執政地位，但可能削弱改革推進速度，造成市場風險溢價上升；另外英國新任首相柏南承諾維
持既有財政規則，並受到工黨不提高所得稅、國民保險及主要稅率承諾的約束，使政府在國防、公共
服務與生活成本支出增加之際 ， 財政操作空間相當有限 ， 市場因此高度關注政府是否轉向其他形式的
增稅。若改革措施過度激進推高英國公債殖利率，恐將造成市場震盪。 
歐洲經濟整體呈現溫和復甦，但原油價格再次走高是最大隱憂。若能源成本居高不下，歐洲央行
與英國央行第四季仍可能維持鷹派立場 ， 進而導致產業表現分化 。 銀行與金融類股可望受惠於利率走
高及淨利差改善；反之，科技、醫療保健及其他成長型類股將面臨殖利率上升所帶來的評價壓力。目
前泛歐 600 指數本益比約 16.5 倍，股價淨值比約 2.5 倍。雖然俄烏戰爭與通膨再度升溫風險仍需關
注，但在評價合理的背景下，歐股仍適合作為長線分批布局標的。

資料來源：彭博,2026/8/31

日本股市：通膨回溫拉高升息預期，半導體與內需動能推升經濟表現
八月日股受地緣政治風險與殖利率走高影響，日經225 指數劇烈震盪。盤面上，強勢族群包括瑞
可利控股、愛德萬測試、藤倉、軟銀、松下控股、村田製作所、瑞穗金融、東京威力科創、三菱UFJ
金融、揖斐電等；弱勢股包括東京海上控股、迅銷、大金工業、富士軟片、日本電裝、發那科、7&I、
丸紅、損保控股、KDDI 等。
8 月東京核心 CPI 升至 1.8%，雖能源補貼與食品價格降溫壓抑整體物價，但租金與醫療服務價
格明顯上升；扣除食品與能源後通膨加速至 1.4%，顯示內生性通膨壓力仍在。若就十大產品分項來

看，引導本月通膨的上漲主要來自房屋及醫療保健，其中租金價格年增幅達 1.9%，反映物價上漲持
續傳遞至房市 ； 醫療保健價格年增幅亦自前月的1.4%攀升至 2.8%， 因政府將高額療養費制度內的自
付費用上限調高所致 。 根據總務省估算 ， 日本政府對能源補貼的政策約壓抑本月通膨0.27 個百分點，
顯示若扣除政策影響，則當前通膨約為 2.2%，仍高於日本央行所設定的物價目標。雖政府補貼政策
將持續對通膨帶來壓抑效果，使得整體通膨略低於 2%，然而搭配央行對日圓匯率過於弱勢、恐進一
步墊高通膨的擔憂，預估將促使日本央行最快於 9 月份升息一碼。
日本半導體製造裝置協會 （SEAJ） 公布7 月日本製晶片設備銷售額 （3 個月移動平均值） 為5,562.2
億日圓，月銷售額年增 35.4%，創下 2024 年 12 月以來最大增幅。同時，若觀察今年 1 至 7 月份累
計銷售額達 3.44 兆日圓，年增 15.7%，顯示隨著當前人工智慧墊高晶片需求，推升廠商對半導體設
備的需求。
日本 7 月外國旅客入境人數為 344.2 萬人次，年增 0.1%。本月份外國旅客人數最大宗仍是來自

南韓，達 89.5 萬人，年增 31.9%。其次則為台灣遊客的 76.4 萬人次，年增 26.4%。與之相反，來
自中國的遊客人數年減幅達 56.1%， 因日中兩國關係遲遲未見改善所致 。 根據日本百貨店協會公布的
資料顯示，7 月百貨公司免稅銷售額年增 25.5%至 506 億日圓，連續五個月的正成長，顯示在日圓
延續弱勢的格局之下，外國旅客消費力仍有提升空間。
2026 年來日股 TOPIX 指數受惠於 AI 題材、日圓貶值、高市擴大財政刺激與強化軍事安全等政
策利多支撐，雖然日圓在政府干預與 BOJ 升息下繼續貶值空間受限，出口企業的匯兌紅利將逐步消
退，但預料日圓接下來仍以區間整理機會較高，並無大幅升值空間。目前 TOPIX 指數本益比約 17.7
倍、股價淨值比約 1.9 倍，評價仍屬合理，可利用市場震盪機會逢低分批布局。

台灣股市：工業生產創高、景氣續亮紅燈，AI 供應鏈撐起製造業擴張動能
8 月台股在 AI 龍頭財報優於預期及全球 AI 資本支出持續擴張帶動下，市場氣氛維持偏多，但受
到 Jackson Hole 會議後市場重新調整聯準會政策預期、美債殖利率攀升及美元轉強影響，指數於高
檔呈現震盪整理。資金面上，高評價 AI 族群漲多後出現輪動，市場開始重新檢視企業未來獲利成長
能否持續支撐評價水準。不過整體而言，AI 資料中心、半導體、先進封裝及伺服器供應鏈需求仍維
持強勁，有助於消化部分評價壓力，並使市場焦點重新回到企業基本面與獲利成長。

當前臺灣經濟仍處於景氣擴張階段。全球 AI 建置需求推動企業設備投資與科技資本支出維持高
檔，相關需求正逐步由晶片擴散至資料中心、網通設備、電力基礎建設及相關零組件。從國際環境觀
察，美國企業投資及私人需求維持韌性，AI 相關支出已成為支撐美國與全球科技景氣的重要力量，
也持續為台灣出口與半導體供應鏈提供結構性成長動能。
展望 9 月，市場關注焦點將由企業財報逐漸轉向總體經濟數據與聯準會政策方向。Jackson Hole
會議後，市場對於聯準會維持較高利率期間的預期升溫，短期可能提高全球金融市場波動。然而，從
企業基本面觀察，NVIDIA 最新展望顯示 AI 需求仍具高度能見度，全球雲端業者與大型企業的 AI 基
礎建設支出並未出現明顯放緩跡象。在此背景下，只要半導體、伺服器及先進封裝產業維持強勁接單
動能，台灣電子產業景氣仍有機會延續高檔表現。
投資策略方面，仍可維持對 AI 供應鏈的正向看法，但須更重視獲利成長與評價合理性。除了先
進製程及先進封裝外，AI 伺服器組裝、散熱、電源管理、高速傳輸、網通設備及記憶體等受惠 AI 基

建擴張之族群，仍具備中長期成長機會。隨著AI 投資逐步由 GPU 擴散至記憶體、網路交換器、光通
訊及系統整合等領域 ， 受惠範圍亦由少數龍頭供應商進一步擴展至整體供應鏈 ， 有助於支撐相關企業
未來營收與獲利成長動能。另一方面，9 月需留意美國 CPI 數據、聯準會利率決策及美債殖利率變化
對高評價科技股的影響；若市場因政策預期、資金輪動或短期獲利了結而出現回檔，較可能屬於多頭
趨勢中的震盪整理，而非基本面反轉。在企業獲利持續成長、AI 資本支出維持高檔及全球科技景氣
延續復甦的背景下 ， 建議布局方向可由單純題材面轉向具備技術門檻 、 訂單能見度及現金流品質較佳
的個股，以提升投資組合在景氣循環與市場波動中的穩健度。

中國股市：硬科技 IPO 熱潮延燒，A 股中報揭露「增收不增利」普遍現象
8 月中國經濟延續「總量承壓、科技產業相對強勢」的分化格局。儘管房地產調整與內需復甦力
道仍有限，但高端製造、人工智慧及數位經濟仍是政策資源與市場資金聚焦方向。在傳統產業景氣復
甦較為緩慢的情況下 ， 科技產業逐漸成為支撐經濟成長的重要動能 ， 顯示中國經濟正持續朝新舊動能
轉換與產業升級方向發展。
科技產業方面，人形機器人已成為近期最受市場關注的新興產業之一。根據中國國家市場監管總
局統計，2026 年上半年人形機器人領域新設企業數量達 11.6 萬家，較 2025 年同期的 7.2 萬家大幅

成長 61.1%，反映資本投入、技術研發與產業參與程度快速提升。隨著人工智慧、大模型及感測技術
進步，人形機器人正逐步由研發驗證邁向商業 化應用階段，產業發展重點也由概念題材轉向量產能
力、產品可靠度及終端需求驗證。
資本市場表現雖顯示科技板塊仍是 A 股資金主要聚焦方向 ， 但企業基本面改善幅度與市場期待之
間仍存在落差。從滬深 300 成分股來看，科技類企業近年營收維持增長，但獲利表現並未同步大幅
提升，反映部分企業仍面臨研發投入增加、產能擴張及市場競爭加劇等壓力。AI、半導體及高端製造
雖持續獲得政策支持與資本關注 ， 但不少企業的估值已提前反映未來成長預期 ， 實際獲利貢獻仍有待
進一步驗證。市場關注焦點也逐漸由政策題材與自主替代概念，轉向訂單能見度、現金流與獲利能力
等基本面指標；若後續營運表現未能跟上市場預期，不排除科技板塊仍面臨評價修正壓力。
展望 9 月，政策方向預計仍以穩增長與培育新質生產力為主軸，人工智慧、人形機器人、高端製
造及半導體等領域仍將是支持重點 。 在整體經濟復甦力道尚未全面擴散的背景下 ， 市場表現預期仍將

呈現結構性分化，資金持續聚焦具成長潛力與技術門檻的科技產業。投資策略上，可留意人形機器人
產業鏈、工業自動化、AI 應用、半導體設備及高端製造等受惠產業升級趨勢之族群；惟部分熱門題
材評價已反映較高成長預期，後續仍須觀察訂單落地、量產進度及獲利改善情況，避免因短期市場情
緒過熱而提高投資風險。

科技類股：AI 基礎建設持續擴張，算力、光通訊與平台生態同步受惠
全球科技產業的核心成長動能仍來自 AI 基礎建設擴張 ， 但市場焦點已逐步由單純GPU 採購轉向
整體資料中心架構升級 。 隨Google 擴大投入光通訊與高速網路技術 ，AI 資料中心正朝向更高頻寬、
更低延遲及更高能效方向發展 。 市場預估800G 至 1.6T 光通訊滲透率將持續提升 ， 推動光模組 、CPO、
交換器及相關供應鏈需求成長，也反映大型雲端服務供應商仍持續強化 AI 基礎建設布局。在此趨勢
下，AI 投資已由單一晶片擴展至網路、儲存、記憶體及先進封裝等完整生態系，帶動相關供應鏈重

新評價。
企業獲利方面，NVIDIA 最新財報再次驗證 AI 投資循環仍具延續性。公司第二季營收與資料中心
業務均大幅優於預期，資料中心網路業務亦維持高速成長，顯示大型企業、雲端服務業者及 AI 模型
開發商對算力需求仍然強勁。同時，管理層對第三季營收及新一代 Vera Rubin 平台維持正向展望，
反映 AI 基礎建設建置並未出現明顯放緩跡象。不過，市場對AI 產業的評價標準已逐漸由需求成長轉
向獲利品質與投資效益，未來資本支出、折舊攤提與現金流變化仍將是投資人關注重點。
從產業鏈角度來看，AI 需求正持續向外擴散。除了 GPU 之外，高速光通訊、網路交換器、高頻
寬記憶體(HBM)、CPU 及資料中心電力架構均成為下一階段重要受惠領域。另一方面，NVIDIA 近期
宣布結合金融機構與雲端服務資源打造大型 AI 基礎設施平台，顯示產業發展正由硬體銷售進一步延
伸至算力服務與平台營運模式。此舉有利強化 AI 產業生態系並提高資本取得效率，但也反映未來競
爭重點將由單純硬體性能逐步轉向平台整合能力、商業模式及資金運用效率。隨著更多企業投入 AI

建設，市場也開始關注高額資本支出是否能持續帶來相應回報，產業獲利表現仍可能出現明顯分化。
展望後續，AI 仍是科技產業最具確定性的投資主軸，但市場關注焦點正由 「建置多少算力」逐步
轉向「創造多少效益」。NVIDIA 新平台推廣進度、Vera Rubin 產品放量、高速光通訊滲透率提升
及 HBM 供給變化，將是觀察下一階段 AI 投資循環的重要指標。雖然大型雲端業者資本支出尚未出
現明顯下修，支撐整體供應鏈需求維持高檔，但隨著資料中心建置成本、能源支出與折舊費用持續增
加 ， 企業獲利能力與現金流表現的重要性亦將提升 。 未來較具競爭優勢的企業 ， 除了掌握核心技術外 ，
更需具備規模化商業模式及資本效率，才能在 AI 產業由高速成長邁向成熟發展的過程中持續創造價
值。

礦業類股：關稅預期有利銅價支撐，戰略庫存需求是潛在利多
8 月以來礦業類股走勢強勁，倫敦金屬交易所銅價也再創歷史新高，這波上漲的主要動能，仍是
來自於我們先前一再提到的美國潛在銅關稅，改變了全球銅庫存的流向 。 由於市場擔心美國未來可能
對精煉銅加徵關稅，交易商提前將銅運往美國，希望在關稅實施前完成進口，導致其他地區能夠取得
的現貨供應相應減少，在供給減少下持續為銅價帶來支撐。
截至 8 月底，美國現行關稅主要涵蓋銅棒、銅線、銅板及銅管等半成品與衍生產品，精煉銅尚未
正式納入課稅範圍，但市場普遍預估美國政府將有可能在 2027 年開始實施精煉銅關稅，帶動交易商

提前採取備貨行動。路透社報導指出，美國2026 年上半年進口約 88.5 萬噸精煉銅，年增約 3%，紐
約商品交易所銅庫存也攀升至約 67.5 萬噸的歷史高點。若將時間拉長至一年觀察，全球三大交易所
合計增加約 49.58 萬噸庫存，其中約 81%集中於紐約商品交易所，顯示全球可見銅庫存雖然上升，
但新增庫存大多被鎖在美國，非美市場銅庫存相對處於較為緊張的狀態。
除了關稅帶動的庫存 失衡外，銅的實際應用需求也具有基本面支撐。彭博行業研究 預估，2026
年全球銅需求將成長 2.4%至 2.7%，增速雖較 2025 年放緩，但電動車、再生能源、資料中心及電網
升級仍將帶動用銅需求上升。其中雖然中國銅需求成長預估也將放緩，但是中國國家電網已規劃 2026
至 2030 年投資 4 兆元人民幣，較前五年增加 44%，以支應人工智慧用電需求。
另一方面，隨著區域銅庫存出現短缺及 AI 帶動用銅需求上升，若政府與企業在未來計畫提高安
全庫存，將可能成為市場尚未充分反映的中長期上漲基礎。花旗研究估算，目前全球精煉銅總庫存約

為 300 萬噸，僅相當於 1.3 個月的全球消費量。若各國政府及大型供應鏈基於國防、能源安全、人
工智慧基礎建設或地緣政治風險 ， 將安全庫存提高至兩個月 ， 市場約需要額外累積 170 萬噸銅庫存；
若提高至三個月，額外需求更將擴大至約 400 萬噸。由於新礦山從開發到投產需要多年，供給無法
在短期內迅速增加，市場只能透過銅價上漲，促使更多廢銅進入市場，同時迫使部分使用者節約用銅
或尋找替代材料。因此，建立庫存的規模愈大、完成時間愈短，銅價需要調整的漲幅也將愈顯著。
展望後市，儘管美國精煉銅關稅目前尚未正式實施，但市場預期帶來的提前備貨潮仍有利短線銅
價支撐，同時全球用銅需求仍維持成長，新礦山供給又難以快速實現，加上政府及企業可能逐步提高
安全庫存 ， 使銅市場的中長期供需結構仍具有支撐 ， 故對具銅價曝險的礦業類股中長期後市仍可正向
看待 ， 然而仍需留意若未來銅關稅未如市場預期實施 ， 當前囤積於美國市場的銅庫存將可能回歸到全
球市場當中，為銅價帶來短線波動。

能源類股：荷莫茲海峽持續受阻，惟實際供應缺口與庫存降幅低於市場預期
8 月以來，美伊衝突持續反覆升降溫，雙方時而釋出談判訊號，時而再度發生軍事衝突，短期內
仍未出現實質進展，荷莫茲海峽通行亦持續受限。然而，海峽受阻並不代表原有運量全數消失，部分
原油仍可經由海運與陸上替代管線出口 ，同時中國石油需求下降及各國政府釋放戰略庫存亦抵銷部分
中斷影響。
彭博行業研究指出，儘管當前荷莫茲海峽受到干擾，但實際並未呈現完全封鎖的狀態，目前仍有
零星油輪持續通行。彭博指出，由於部分油輪會關閉公開定位訊號，並在通過海峽後於阿曼外海進行

轉運，因此公開船舶資料可能會漏算部分運量。彭博綜合目前庫存變化與市場實際供需後，估計目前
每日仍有約 750 萬桶原油通過荷莫茲海峽，意味僅依據可觀察船舶數量推算中東石油供應中斷量，
可能高估了市場實際缺口。
除了每日仍有約 750 萬桶原油經過海峽運輸外，彭博估計目前另有每日約 500 萬桶石油改走陸
上管線；需求端則因中國每日減少約 400 萬桶進口而下降，其他地區減少用油與各國政府釋放戰略
庫存再合計填補每日約 200 萬桶石油缺口。經過上述調整後，原本每日約 2,000 萬桶經荷莫茲海峽
通行的運量中，實際供應缺口僅剩每日約 150 萬桶，這也解釋了當前油價漲幅為何未完全反映表面
的產量損失。
全球石油庫存變化的數據亦呼應了實際缺口低於市場普遍預期的論點。 花旗報告指出 ，2026 年 2
月至 8 月全球實際觀察到的石油供需缺口平均約為每日 290 萬桶，明顯低於其原先預估的每日 700
萬至 800 萬桶。截至 2026 年 8 月，全球石油庫存仍可覆蓋 99 天需求，亦仍處於 2022 年至 2025

年的 95 至 101 天區間。花旗估計，若目前庫存下降速度延續，全球庫存要到 2029 年第一季才會降
至接近第二次石油危機緊張程度的 70 天水位，這顯示荷莫茲海峽若持續受阻，雖油價上漲壓力仍會
逐步累積，但實際庫存下降速度並未如市場普遍預期的嚴峻。
展望後市， 除荷莫茲海峽中斷造成的 供應缺口與庫存降幅低於市場預期 外，國際能源署 也預估
2027 年全球石油需求將增加每日 240 萬桶 ， 供應則可能年增每日830 萬桶 ， 意味若中東產量與海峽
通行逐步恢復，供應回升速度將明顯快於需求，並帶動庫存回補，不利油價上漲趨勢，然而考量短線
地緣風險仍有利能源類股形成支撐，在美伊達成長期停火協議前，應中性看待能源類股後市表現。

### Page 10

黃金類股：央行買盤支撐中長期價格，惟升息預期帶動短線波動升高
8 月金價走勢先揚後抑。月初公布的美國就業數據偏弱，加上中國及全球黃金 ETF 買盤回升，使
市場一度降低近期升息預期，金價因而突破 7 月的整理區間。到了 8 月中旬，美國財政部宣布擴大
長天期公債回購，帶動長端殖利率與美元回落，進一步放大黃金漲勢；不過，月底公布的通膨數據仍
顯示物價壓力具有黏性 ， 聯準會主席華許在傑克森霍爾會議的態度也偏向鷹派 ， 使市場重新提高升息
預期，金價隨即自高檔回落。然而即使月底出現修正，金價在 8 月仍累積明顯漲幅。
支撐金價中長期走勢的核心力量，仍然來自世界各國央行為分散外匯儲備，持續增持黃金。世界

黃金協會指出，2026 年第 2 季全球央行淨購金量為 289 噸，累計 2026 年上半年約 345 噸。雖然上
半年央行購金量受到土耳其、俄羅斯等國調節部位影響，降至2022 年以來同期低點，但第 2 季購金
量已較第 1 季明顯回升，而且買盤仍分布於波蘭、中國、烏茲別克及哈薩克等多個市場，顯示各國央
行增持黃金的趨勢仍未出現轉變，且可觀察到各國央行對於逢低承接黃金的意願。
在央行需求之外，中國投資資金是本輪金價突破的重要邊際買盤。2026 年上半年中國黃金淨進
口量達 764 噸，較 2025 年同期增加 138%，其規模明顯高於中國人民銀行公布的增持量，顯示民間
與機構投資人也在提高黃金配置 。 當中國資金推動金價突破整理區間後 ， 美國商品期貨交易委員會的
資料也顯示 ， 管理基金黃金淨多單在 8 月持續增加 ， 使原本集中於亞洲的漲勢進一步獲得美國期貨資
金支持。
然而，投資資金參與程度提高，也使金價對利率與美元變化更加敏感。美國財政部 8 月 19 日宣
布，將至少加倍 10 年至 30 年期公債的流動性支持回購規模，市場隨即下調長端殖利率，黃金也在

持有成本下降後快速上漲。不過，月底公布的美國 7 月通膨數據仍高，加上華許在傑克森霍爾會議維
持偏鷹立場，再次使市場重新認為聯準會仍可能透過升息抑制通膨，美元與殖利率回升後，部分短線
資金便出現獲利了結賣壓。
展望後市，各國央行增持黃金儲備仍支撐黃金的中長期多頭結構，而美國財政與債務可持續性疑
慮也將持續提高黃金的分散風險價值。不過，8 月底的回檔顯示，當金價漲勢由 ETF 及期貨資金放大
後，實質利率與美元只要轉為上升，短線市場部位便可能迅速調整，故在美國後續利率政策仍具有不
確定性的狀況下，對於黃金類股後市應中性看待。

### Page 11

任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本
身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同意，
不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
固定收益：長端風險仍存，3~10 年期公債、投資級債更具表現機會
8 月以來，美國公債殖利率維持高檔震盪，若以 7 月底與 8/24 比較，各年期債券殖利率近乎平
行小幅下行。8 月初公布的 7 月非農就業意外減少 2.3 萬人，顯示勞動市場動能轉弱；7 月 CPI 年增
率由 3.5%降至 3.4%，核心 CPI 年增率亦由 2.6%降至 2.5%，一度緩解市場對 Fed 緊縮疑慮。惟 7
月 PPI 與 PCE 數據表現，限制利率進一步下行空間。
8 月中旬之後，市場焦點由政策利率逐步轉向能源價格、財政供給與期限溢酬。美伊局勢反覆推

升油價，加上美國聯邦債務8 月首度突破 40 兆美元，2 年與 30 年期利差由 7 月底 99 個基點擴大至
8/18 的 109 個基點，30 年期殖利率亦升至 5.28%。美國財政部 8/19 宣布，自 9/9 起將部分 10～
30 年期公債單次流動性回購上限由 20 億美元提高至至少 40 億美元，帶動 30 年期殖利率由 5.28%
快速回落至 5.19%。截至 8/24，2 年與 30 年利差亦回到 99 個基點，月中陡峭化大致回吐。此一走
勢顯示 ，回購措施雖有助改善長券流動性 ， 但財政赤字 、公債供給及期限溢酬等結構性壓力仍未消失 。
全球主要成熟國家的長端債券亦面臨類似壓力。德國 30 年期公債殖利率近期升至約 3.79%，創
2011 年以來最高；日本 10 年期公債殖利率 8/18 盤中一度觸及 2.945%，亦為 1996 年 9 月以來高
位，30 年期亦一度升至 4.115%。成熟市場長債同步承壓，反映財政擴張、通膨風險與貨幣政策正常
化正共同推升長端期限溢酬。

不過，信用市場整體仍相對穩定，截至 8/24，聖路易聯準銀行的 ICE 美國銀行美國投資級公司
債 OAS 約 81 個基點、有效殖利率約 5.4%，但信用利差仍處歷史偏低區間，緩衝並不寬裕。AI 基礎
建設融資的供給壓力亦已開始反映在定價，Reuters 8/21 指出，2026 年至 8 月上旬主要 AI 雲端業
者發債規模已約 2,200 億美元，科技類投資級債利差升至 89 個基點，較整體 IG 市場高出約 9 個基
點；近期 Alphabet 新券亦需提供約 10～15 個基點讓利，顯示在企業基本面仍穩健之際，大量新券
供給已成為影響信用評價的重要因素。
展望 9 月，美債殖利率預料仍將處於相對高檔，若就業持續降溫且通膨未再明顯升高，中期利率
上行空間可望受限。期限配置方面，短端對 Fed 政策預期較敏感，過短存續期間亦有再投資風險；
10 年以上債券則持續承受財政與期限溢酬壓力。值得注意的是，截至 8/27，10 年與 30 年期殖利率
利差為 52 個基點，2 年至 30 年總利差 96 個基點，顯示 2 年至 30 年期殖利率曲線陡峭幅度中，逾

半來自 10~30 年區間，反映超長端相對 10 年期仍有明顯期限溢酬。相較之下，3～10 年期兼具較
高息收、適度存續期間與利率下行參與空間。整體而言，目前 3～10 年期美國公債及投資級債的風
險報酬條件相對均衡，料具表現機會。

Taiwan Semis: GUC (3443.TW): Mgmt. Call takeaways: Server CPU ramp 
to sustain momentum into 2027; opportunities across applications
31 October 2026 | 12:35AM HKT
We talked to GUC (3443.TW, not covered) management recently. The company’s Sep 
revenues were up 22% MoM (vs. +1% MoM in Aug), reflecting its revenues from CPU 
of a leading US CSP in strong ramp up with more foundry capacity rolling out, and 
would continue to support the growth in 4Q26 and 2027 per the company. The 
positive tone echoes our positive view on Global Servers TAM, which we expect 
leading US cloud service providers (CSPs) in aggregate will grow their capex budgets 
by 84%/ 52%/ 13% YoY in 2026-28E, with our estimates being raised by 4% / 17% / 
22% in 2026-28E to US$1,397bn by 2028E. Revenues in 3Q26 are driven by both 
NRE and turnkey solutions, with turnkey solutions revenues up 44% QoQ on the CPU 
ramp up, and despite turnkey solutions up to ~90% of 3Q26 revenues, the GM 
sustained at 20%+ in 3Q26, thanks to higher-margin NRE milestones. GUC adjusts 
pricing to reflect rising wafer and memory costs, and management sees memory 
impact is manageable on its customers’ projects. On competition, management 
highlighted its position as a core partner of TSMC, with in-house IP for 3D stacking as 
a key differentiator. GUC’s strong turnkey pull-in from its CSP customer’s server CPU 
and its focus on IP for TSMC 3D stacking point to continued demand for TSMC’s 
(2330.TW, Buy) advanced nodes and packaging. The positive tone on ASIC demand 
also echoes our positive view on rising ASIC penetration rate (~60% by 2028E, report 
link), supported by leading CSPs diversifying thier AI chips platforms and reducing 
token per second per costs, driving inferencing / training efficiency.

Company profile: GUC (3443.TW, not covered) is a ASIC design service company 
and a TSMC affiliate, providing NRE design services and turnkey production for

### Page 2

custom chips manufactured at TSMC. Its customers span cloud (CPUs, AI accelerators,
BMCs), automotive and consumer (image sensors, SSD controllers), with 68% of its
products under 7nm.
Key takeaways
1. Mass production as the key profit driver: Management noted that the key to
profitability lies in converting NRE projects into mass production. With the largest
customer’s CPU now in volume production, turnkey has become the main growth driver.
NRE carries higher GM than turnkey, but as it is recognized by milestone, quarterly NRE

margin varies with the mix of projects at different stages.
2. Expanding opportunities across cloud and automotive: GUC continues to open up
new opportunities across applications. In cloud, it works with all major US CSPs on
projects spanning CPUs and GPUs and also supplies other server projects such as BMCs,
while actively pursuing further AI accelerator opportunities. In automotive, it is engaged
with both US and Chinese customers at the NRE stage, two of which already plan for

mass production.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

## Document 158: 261104_daiwa_guc

Source PDF: all report pdf/261104_daiwa_guc.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-04
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: Following its results release on 30 October, we raise our 
forecasts and 12M TP for Global Unichip (GUC). The stock more than 
doubled since our last update (Upgrading: unleashed capacity adding to 
growth, 5 August), yet its recent pullback amid increased market volatility 
looks to be unlocking value again for investment, so we reaffirm our Buy (1) 
rating. Fundamentals have unfolded strongly. According to our market 
research, GUC’s key customer in the AI CPU space has pulled forward its 
order trajectory ahead of the previous schedule amid accelerated demand 
for internal and external AI datacentre build-out. This comes on top of its 
initial ramp of another new accelerator project, followed by the next 
generation AI CPU and accelerator ramps for the same customer, with 
further upside from another new AI accelerator customer in the auto space. 
We suggest investors take advantage of the pullback to bargain-hunt.

What’s the impact: Forecast changes. In light of its key AI CPU customer 
accelerating its ordering trajectory, together with multiple AI projects 
ramping in the next 1-2 years to fire on all cylinders for growth, we lift our 
2026-28E EPS by 8-50% for GUC. We forecast triple-digit % YoY EPS 
growth over 2Q26-2Q27, before moderating to 50% or so into 2028. While 
such strong growth should help sustain its hefty valuation, further rerating 
may be unlikely as this would depend on GUC’s ability to win more AI 
projects than we expect, and more importantly, whether it is able to secure 
enough capacity from the supply chain to accommodate any incremental 
business amid the constrained supply environment.

Margin-accretive at operating level. On top of the AI CPU demand uplift, 
we expect GUC to have a total of 6 major AI projects in mass-production for 
2027, including 4 CPU and 2 accelerators, all for US-based hyperscalers or 
system vendors mainly focusing on N3 process nodes, helping broaden 
GUC’s AI project base. Although these projects appear margin-dilutive at 
gross level, they should be accretive at the operating level, thanks to 
GUC’s prudent opex management on enhanced operating leverage.

What we recommend: Given our forecast changes, we lift our 12M TP for 
GUC to TWD7,800 (previous: TWD5,125), based on a higher 4-quarter 
forward PER target of 67x (previous: 63x), justified by its 2026-28E EPS 
CAGR of 66% from a PEG perspective. Although further rerating remains 
challenging, we are comfortable with our Buy (1) call due to the recent 
share price pullback unlocking value for investment again. Key downside 
risks to our call would be supply constraints and margin execution.

How we differ: Our 2027-28E EPS are 23-37% below the consensus 
forecasts as we factor in the supply constraints that will moderate growth.

4 November 2026

Value returns when jitters rise

➢ Lifting forecasts on AI strength and a broadened project base 
➢ Margin dilutive at gross level yet accretive at operating level 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) with a higher 12M TP of TWD7,800

Company profile

### Page 5

Global Unichip (3443 TT): 4 November 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 5 May 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 1
GUC's Board of Directors (BoD) comprises 8 members, including 4 independent directors (50%
of the board), which is significantly higher than the regulatory requirement of at least 20%. The
Board demonstrates diverse expertise across legal, finance, technology, and business
management. The Chairman and the President are different individuals, ensuring separation of
roles and enhancing governance independence.
Capital management 1
Over the past decade, GUC has maintained a consistently high dividend payout ratio, generally
above 60%, and in most years exceeding 80%. In 2025, the company distributed TWD2,144m in
cash dividends, reflecting its policy of sustaining or increasing dividend amounts over time.
Given GUC’s asset-light business model as an IC design service provider, which does not
require significant capital expenditure for manufacturing facilities, this high payout ratio is
appropriate and aligns with its long-term commitment to shareholder returns.
Related party &
transaction 1
GUC's sole foundry supplier is TSMC, who is also GUC's largest shareholder. As an IC design
service company without onsite production, GUC's relationship with the leading foundry of
advanced process nodes provides it a key competitive advantage, in our view.
S Supply chain management 2
GUC is committed to maintaining long-term partnerships with suppliers and establishing a
sustainable supply chain through measures such as promoting its green action policy and
concept to the entire supply chain, where all its suppliers are required to follow its Responsible
Business Alliance (RBA) regulation. It also periodically conducts supplier audits to ensure the
quality of suppliers.
E Water & wastewater management 1
GUC continues its green living project to reduce water consumption. In 2024, the Company
maintained water-saving measures such as rainwater collection systems, automatic sensor
faucets, dual-flush toilets, and regular inspections of water equipment. These efforts aim to
improve water efficiency and promote conservation across operations.
E
Waste & hazardous
materials
management
1
As an IC design service provider, GUC does not have in-house manufacturing facilities. It
requires its suppliers to use recycled materials for on-site operation and transportation, and
reduce the amount of expendable resources being used. In order to promote a paperless office,
GUC has established a cloud operating system to reduce the use of paper-based documents.
E GHG emissions 1
GUC adopts energy-saving measures to reduce greenhouse gas emissions and has set annual
electricity conservation targets. In 2024, the Company achieved a 1% reduction in electricity use
and increased green electricity to 39% of total consumption. It also continues to develop low-
power and high-efficiency IP to help minimise environmental impact.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

### Page 1

King Yuan Electronics Corp.

Testing business diversification strengthen 
’27 outlook at low-end valuation

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 330.00 TWD | Price: 228.50 TWD

3Q26 results in line; 4Q TPU ramp offsets GPU headwind 
3Q26 sales were +9% QoQ backed by healthy demand from its GPU/CPU customers and 
early mobile inventory builds. With higher depreciation and labor cost moving to COGS 
as new facility production kick off, GM dipped 190bps QoQ to 37.6% in 3Q26 but 
operating leverage lifted OpM 40bps QoQ to 25.8%, taking EPS to NT$2.06. 4Q26 sales 
is guided +7-9% QoQ, in line with our preview, due to slower GPU ramp from continued 
HBM4 yield issue at Hynix partially offsetting strength in AI ASICs (Google’s v8t/i TPUs) 
and CPUs (Nvidia Vera, Google Axion and Microsoft Cobalt). Despite better business mix, 
the rising cost and slower UTR improvement should keep GMs stable at 36-37% in 4Q. 
Solid position into 2027 across chipset and wafer testing 
KYEC’s ’26 sales is now on track to grow 34% YoY and could further accelerate in ’27 at 
+53% YoY from a diversifying application base across GPU, CPU, networking switches and 
ASICs. It is also in a strong position to capture its foundry partner’s chip probing 
outsourcing demand in addition to higher testing unit/dollar content from its existing 
direct customer business. To support expansion across wafer sorting, chip testing and 
system level testing, the company is accelerating its clean room construction in Taiwan 
and has announced it will grow its testing footprint in the US. While its ’27 capex could 
stay at elevated levels, we think the investment mix shifting toward facility should keep its 
depreciation under control. This along with capex-light tool consignment business model 
could drive its GMs to 40%+ in 1H27 and keep profitability resilient through the cycles. 
Recent pullback to low-end valuation: particularly attractive 
We keep EPS for ‘26/27/28E largely unchanged at NT$7.87/NT$15.36/NT$19.18, and 
reiterate our Buy rating with PO of NT$330 still based on 18x 2H27-1H28E P/E (vs. its 
10-25x P/E range). Following the AI sentiment drag, the stock is trading at 15x/11x 
‘27/28E P/E, attractive as the business downside is protected by its leadership in scale 
and execution for AI chipset testing even if AI investment moderates in ‘28.

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
Founded in 1987 and headquartered in Hsinchu, Taiwan, 
King Yuan Electronics Corp. (KYEC) is a niche Outsourced 
Semiconductor Assembly and Test (OSAT) company 
focused on IC testing services, with market shares of 3% in 
OSAT (IC packaging+testing, number 7 vendor) and 14% in 
IC testing (number 2 vendor) globally. Its capacity is mainly 
located in Greater China. In addition to IC testing, it has 
c.20% revenue exposure to IC packaging, mainly at its 
Chinese subsidiary and mainly for memory products.

Investment Rationale 
We have a Buy rating on KYEC, in view of undervalued 
upside from smartphone business for both near-term and 
longer-term, structural opportunities in AI areas led by GPU, 
as well as the solid GPM/ROE expansion toward into 2025.

### Page 5

Price objective basis & risk 
King Yuan Electronics Corp. (KYUFF) 
We value KYEC at NT$330 per share, based on 18x 2H27-1H28E P/E, which is close to 
its historical average since 2010. In our view, P/E valuation is appropriate for KYEC given 
its strong growth in earnings over the past few years, future growth potentials with 
upside in total addressable market (TAM) and market share, and enhanced cash position. 
We think 18x P/E, which is above the average P/E level of global OSAT peers, is 
undemanding, in view of its better ROE profile and 2-3% dividend yield.

Downside risks are 
1) faster-than-expected pick-up by testing peers in high-end AI area, 
2) greater-than-expected slowdown in global smartphone/consumer electronics demand, 
3) potential asset disposal caused by strategy shift amid the ongoing decoupling in the 
semis supply chain.

Nan Ya PCB  | Asia Pacific
In-line 3Q26 gross margin
3Q26 results:
• 3Q revenue was NT$16.4bn (+21% q/q, +49% y/y).
• GM reached 29.3% (+460bps q/q, +1,970bps y/y), slightly above our estimate
but in line with the Street, driven by higher ABF (low 30s  %) and BT GM
(high 20s  %).  PCB remains  loss-making on the gross margin level.
• OP was NT$4.3bn (+49% q/q, +597% y/y), 1% above our estimate and the
Street, for OpM of 26.4% (+500bps q/q, +2,070bps y/y).

• However, non-op income was below our estimate and the Street. Tax rate of
~26% was again higher than our estimate at 16% and the Street at 19%.
• Thus, NP came in at NT$3.3bn (+46% q/q, +355% y/y), 13% below our
estimate/8% below the Street, for EPS of  NT$5.10.

Key Takeaways
NYPCB's 3Q GM is  largely in line with both buy-side and sell-side estimates.
The 500bps sequential improvement in OpM looks positive to us. Similar to

Unimicron's 3Q results, we view NYPCB's slight miss as an expectation
mismatch...
...rather than reflecting any structural concerns.
We also expect NYPCB to gain share for ABF substrates for AMD's server CPUs,
as one Japanese competitor reallocates more capacity to another CPU customer.
We remain constructive on all ABF substrate stocks in our coverage.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Unimicron (3037.TW)
Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate
value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.2%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 14%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF substrate demand from PC and server customers

n Capex cuts; halt to its capacity expansion plan
n Continued yield issues of alternative technology that doesn't require substrate (e.g.,
CoWoP)
Risks to Downside
n Sudden demand shortfall
n Technological change that would not require ABF substrates
n Intensifying competition
n Yield issues or production hiccups when ramping new capacity
Nan Ya PCB (8046.TW)
Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate

[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.

below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

## Document 214: [000660] SK하이닉스(000660) 여전히 강력하다SK하이닉스(000660) 여전히 강력하다여전히 강력하다 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[000660] SK하이닉스(000660) 여전히 강력하다SK하이닉스(000660) 여전히 강력하다여전히 강력하다 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

00

SK 하이닉스 (000660/KS) 
여전히 강력하다 
3Q26 영업이익 83 조원으로 예상 상회 
SK하이닉스의 3Q26 영업이익은 83조원 (+38% QoQ)으로 예상을 상회했다. 메모
리 공급 부족 심화, HBM4 반영 시작에 따른 메모리 가격 상승이 환율 하락을 상쇄했
기 때문이다. 출하 DRAM +10%, NAND -1%, 가격 DRAM +28%, NAND 
+24%, 영업이익률 DRAM 82%, NAND 72%, HBM 판가는 HBM4 출하가 시작되
며 37% 수준 상승한 것으로 추정된다. 업황은 여전히 예상을 상회하고 있다.

4Q26 분기 영업이익 100 조원 시대 진입 전망 
4Q26 영업이익은 105조원 (+27% QoQ)으로 분기 100조원 시대에 진입할 것으로 
전망한다. 메모리 가격 강세가 지속되는 가운데, 1c 출하 가속화, M15X 램프업 본격화 
등으로 출하량 상승이 기존 예상을 상회할 것이다. 또한 HBM4의 Cross-over 시작 
및 수율 안정화로 HBM 가격, 수익성 제고 동반이 지속될 것이다. 출하는 DRAM 
+24%, NAND +4%, 가격은 DRAM +8%, NAND +7%를 예상한다.

2027 년 영업이익 488 조원 (+70% YoY)으로 상향조정 
SK하이닉스의 2027년 영업이익을 488조원 (+70% YoY)으로 12% 상향 조정한
다. 원/달러 환율 전망치를 기존 1,340원에서 1,315원으로 하향 조정했음에도, 공급 
부족 심화 속 2027년향 HBM 가격 인상 및 예상을 상회하는 수율, 1Q27 진행될 모바
일 DRAM 가격 인상 등을 반영했기 때문이다. LTA 가격 역시 개별 약정된 범위 내 
시장 상황을 반영할 수 있는 구조라는 점을 감안하면, 지속 상승할 것이라 전망한다.

높은 주주환원 의지 확인, 높아진 실적 가시성, 지나친 저평가 
투자의견 매수, 목표주가 4,000,000원을 유지한다. SK하이닉스는 LTA에 대해 기
존 5년에서 추가 연장에 대한 논의가 진행 중이며, 2029년까지 HBM, DRAM은 사실
상 완판, HBM 수요는 공급 가능 물량의 2 배를 상회, 2030년까지 메모리 수요 우위 
국면 전망을 밝혔다. AI 인프라 투자의 강력한 명분과 메모리 수요의 구조성, 잠재적으
로 매우 높은 LTA 위반 비용을 감안하면 실적 가시성과 안정성 제고가 동반되는 국면
이다. 100조원 수준의 주주환원은 2026년 예상 FCF의 66% 수준, 분기별 자사주 
매입, 소각 지속 계획을 통해 높은 주주환원 의지를 보여주고 있다. 실적 Duration 장
기화, 주주환원을 감안하면 12MF P/E 3.4X 수준의 현 주가는 현저한 저평가 국면이
다. 과도한 우려의 되돌림과 메모리 밸류에 대한 재논의 국면 진입을 전망한다.

2026-10-29

본 보고서에 언급된 종목의 경우 당사 조사분석담당자는 본인의 담당종목을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.

당사는 자료공표일 현재 해당기업과 관련하여 특별한 이해 관계가 없습니다. 
종목별 투자의견은 다음과 같습니다.

3Q26 전사 예상 영업이익 100조원 
DS부문 예상 영업이익은 100.0조원(OPM 67.4%, YoY +1538.5%, QoQ +14.5%) 으로, 
달러환율 1,380원기준, 메모리와 비메모리 사업부문 예상 영업손익은 각각 102.0조원,    
-2.0조원으로 추정한다. 1) 메모리반도체 가격 상승세가 이어지는 가운데, HBM4 판매 
본격화에 따른 영향을 고려하면 DRAM/NAND 분기 평균 가격 상승폭은 각각 +18.0%, 
+16.0%으로 추정하고, 2)비메모리반도체 사업 적자는 지속될 전망이기 때문이다. 다만 
특별 성과급 지급에 따른 충당금 영향을 제외하고 선단공정 중심의 파운드리 가동률을 
고려하면 적자폭은 유의미하게 감소한 것으로 파악된다는 점이 긍정적이다.   
SDC부문 예상 영업이익은 1.1조원(OPM 13.0%, YoY -9.6%, QoQ +54.9%)으로, 1)국내 
주력 고객사향 폴더블 스마트폰 판매 호조가 사업부의 외형 성장에 긍정적으로 작용했을 
것으로 추정됨에도 불구하고, 2) 8.6세대 IT OLED 패널 양산 개시에 따른 감가상각비(약 
0.3조원 수준)와 북미 고객 중심의 판가 인하 압박 영향으로 전년동기대비 수익성은 약 
2%pt 훼손이 불가피할 전망이다. DX부문 예상 영업손익은 -1.1조원(YoY 적자전환, QoQ 
적자지속)으로, 메모리반도체를 포함한 IT 부품 가격 부담이 전분기대비 확대되고 있기 
때문이다. MX와 VD/DA사업부 예상 손익은 각각 -0.9조원, -0.2조원으로 추정한다.

투자의견 BUY, 목표주가 63만원으로 상향 조정  
메모리반도체의 가격 상승폭이 다소 둔화되겠지만, 2027~2028년에도 타이트한 수급 
환경이 이어질 전망이라는 점을 주목한다. 1)시장은 신규 증설과 HBM 관련 노이즈로 인한 
2028년 피크아웃 가능성을 우려하고 있으나, HBM 의 범용 DRAM 생산능력 잠식과 AI 
추론 확산에 따른 고용량 메모리 수요를 감안하면 공급 제약의 해소 시점은 예상보다 
늦어질 가능성이 높기 때문이다. 뿐만 아니라, 2)메모리 업사이클이 장기화될 경우 동사 
FCF는 과거대비 구조적으로 확대되어 보다 친화적인 주주환원 정책(2027~2029년, 
2027년 1월 예정)을 제시할 가능성도 배제할 수 없기 때문이다. 동사 2027년 연간 실적 
추정치 상향 조정에 따라 목표주가를 63만원(2027년 BPS * Target PBR 3.5배 적용)으로 
상향 조정한다. 피크아웃 우려에 따른 주가 조정은 중장기 관점에서 매수 기회다.

주가 추이

3분기 영업 이 익 은 지상방산 매출 감소와 일 회성 비용 등의 영 향 으로 
기대치를 대폭 하회했습니다. 4분기 실적 회복 과 중 장기 실적 성장 대
비 저평가된 밸 류 에이 션 에 주목합 니 다. 
3Q26 Review: 영업이익 0.5조원으로 컨센서스 대폭 하회 
3분기 매출액은 6.1조원으로 전년대비 6.0% 감소했다. 항공우주 부문
과 연결자회사들 성장에도 불구하고 지상방산 매출이 전년대비 약 0.9
조원 감소한 영향이다. 지상방산 내수와 수출 매출은 각각 0.7조원, 0.6
조원으로 전년대비 28.6%, 50.4% 감소했다. 내수는 대전 사업장 생산 
중단, 수출은 4분기 납기 집중에 기인한다. 항공우주 매출은 군수사업 
물량 증가로 전년대비 9.3% 증가한 0.7조원을 기록했다. 연결 자회사
인 한화오션과 한화시스템 매출은 각각 3.4조원, 0.8조원으로 전년대비 
12.9%, 30.1% 증가했다. 영업이익은 0.5조원으로 전년대비 49.2% 감
소했으며 영업이익률은 7.4%로 6.3%P 하락했다. 한화오션과 한화시스
템의 이익 증가에도 지상방산 이익 감소와 필리조선소 적자 반영이 영
향을 미쳤다. 지상방산은 일회성 비용 약 460억원, 원화 강세, 매출 감
소에 따른 고정비 부담 증가가 복합적으로 작용했다. 세전이익은 한화
오션의 외화환산손실 영향으로 -0.2조원을 기록하며 적자전환했다.  
실적 회복과 저평가 매력에 주목할 시점 
4분기 영업이익은 1.7조원으로 추정하며 시장 기대치를 상회할 전망이
다. 지상방산 수출 인도 물량이 집중되는 영향이다. 2027년 예상 실적 
기준 P/E는 12.5배로 경쟁사 Rheinmetall(22.5배) 대비 낮다. 약 35조
원의 방산 수주잔고를 기반으로 최소 2029년까지 실적 성장이 예상되
는 만큼 저평가 매력이 높다. 신규 수주 시점과 실적에 대한 우려가 상
존하지만 현재 밸류에이션에는 상당 부분 반영된 것으로 판단한다. 미
국 중간선거 등 국방예산 관련 정책 불확실성과 환율 부담이 완화되면 
투자자들의 관심은 4분기 실적과 2027년 신규 수주로 이동할 전망이
다. 연말 주요국의 국방예산 확정은 신규 수주 시점에 대한 불확실성을 
낮추는 계기가 될 수 있다.  
투자의견 BUY, 목표주가 1,300,000원 유지 
글로벌 재무장 수요 확대에 따른 동사의 중장기 성장 전망은 여전히 
유효하다. 투자의견 BUY와 목표주가 1,300,000원을 유지한다.

[ Compliance Notice ] (공표일: 2026 년 10 월 30 일) 
이 자료는 조사분석 담당자가 객관적 사실에 근거해 작성하였으며, 타인의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 
반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (채운샘) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
한화에어로스페이스는 당사와 ‘독점규제 및 공정거래에 관한 법률’ 에 따른 계열회사 관계에 있는 법인입니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 6

한화에어로스페이스 (012450) [한화리서치]

6 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

[ 당사 조사분석자료의 투자등급 부여 비중 ] (기준일: 2026 년 9 월 30 일)

## Document 237: [012450] 한화에어로스페이스(012450) 일시적 부진, 4Q부터 성장 가도에 재진입한화에어로스페이스(012450) 일시적 부진, 4Q부터 성장 가도에_b94e5eb0

Source PDF: all report pdf/[012450] 한화에어로스페이스(012450) 일시적 부진, 4Q부터 성장 가도에 재진입한화에어로스페이스(012450) 일시적 부진, 4Q부터 성장 가도에_b94e5eb0.pdf

### Page 1

한화에어로스페이스 (012450/KS) 
일시적 부진, 4Q부터 성장 가도에 재진입 
2026-10-30

조선/방산. 한승한, RA. 고서영 / shane.han@sks.co.kr / 3773-9992

매수(유지) 
목표주가: 1,300,000 원(하향) 
현재주가: 760,000 원 
상승여력: 71.1%

Signal: 3Q26, 수출 물량 저조 및 국내 낮은 마진 물량 인식으로 어닝 쇼크 
Key: 4Q부터 내년까지 수출 물량 빠르게 회복하며 실적 성장 이어갈 전망 
Step: 연내 새로운 중장기 성장동력이 될 중동 수주계약 기대. 매수 기회

3Q26 Review: 수출 물량 납품 저조로 어닝 쇼크  
3Q26 연결 매출액 6 조 943 억원(-6.0% YoY, -34.4% QoQ), 영업이익 4,520억원
(-46.2% YoY, -66.9% QoQ)으로, 시장예상치(1조 1,615억원)를 대폭 하회하는 어닝 쇼
크 기록했다. ①폴란드 중심의 수출 물량의 매출 인식 시점이 4 분기로 이연되며, 수출 물량 
비중이 감소한 점이 컸다. ②내수 물량 또한 감소했으며, 특히 국내 물량 중에서도 상대적
으로 마진율 낮은 유무인 무기체계 초도 물량이 이번 분기에 집중됐다. ③내수&수출 전반
적인 지상방산 매출 비중 감소에 따른 고정비 부담이 컸으며, ④일회성 요인으로 대전 사업
장 사고에 따른 복구 비용 약 300 억원과 충당금 및 기타 비용 총 160 억원, 그리고 환율 
하락으로 영업이익의 약 10% 수준의 감소 효과 발생했다. 다만 4Q26 부터 이번 분기 이
연된 폴란드 중심의 수출 물량 인식이 반영되면서 큰 폭의 탑라인 및 이익 성장 시현할 전
망이며, 대전 사업장 또한 내년 상반기 가동 재개 예상됨에 따라 지상방산 부문은 전년 대비 
성장 폭 두드러지며 사측 가이던스 15% 매출 성장 충분히 달성 가능할 전망이다.

투자의견 ‘매수’ 유지, 목표주가 130 만원으로 하향 
한화에어로스페이스에 대한 투자의견 ‘매수’를 유지하고, 목표주가 기존 160 만원에서 130
만원으로 하향한다. 최근 Peer 그룹의 주가 하락에 따라 멀티플 하향 조정했기 때문이다. 
폴란드 천무 유도탄 JV에 이어 현지화가 전제된 K9 3차 계약을 추진 중이며, 국내 장약 생
산능력 증설을 통한 유럽향 수요 대응과 미국 내 생산거점 구축까지 진행 중에 있다. 또한 
미국 차륜형 자주포 사업 시제 선정에 이은 궤도형 사업 수주까지 기대할 수 있다. 중동 국
가들의 방공무기체계에 대한 계약 논의는 매우 가시적인 상황이며, 사우디 MNG 사업 수주 
또한 시점의 문제일 뿐이다. 기존 유럽에 더해 중동은 현지화 전략을 기반으로 동사의 새로
운 중장기 성장동력이 될 지역이며, 올해 4 분기부터 해당 수주 모멘텀과 함께 큰 폭의 실적 
성장이 맞물리며 리레이팅 가능할 전망이다. 선제적 매수 기회라는 판단이다.

## Document 268: AI・半導体で韓国景気は堅調も、「Ｋ字型」の様相が一段と鮮明に ～家計部門の低迷が李政権の重しとなる可能性～

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI・半導体で韓国景気は堅調も、「Ｋ字型」の様相が一段と鮮明に ～家計部門の低迷が李政権の重しとなる可能性～.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Asia Trends

2026 年10 月 27 日

1/5 
AI・半導体で韓国景気は堅調も、「Ｋ字型」の様相が一段と鮮明に 
～家計部門の低迷が李政権の重しとなる可能性～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ 韓国景気は、世界的なＡＩ・半導体関連需要を追い風に、外需主導で堅調な推移が続いてい
る。 ７-９月の実質ＧＤＰ成長率は前期比＋1.0％ （年率換算＋3.93％） 、 前年同期比＋3.4％
となり、2026 年９月までの累計成長率（＋3.6％）は中銀の８月時点の見通し（＋3.3％）を
上回った。メモリ半導体を中心とする財輸出に加え、半導体製造設備やデータセンター関連
の設備投資が成長を支え、2027 年も外需の堅調さが景気をけん引する展開が見込まれる。 
○ 一方、家計部門は力強さを欠いている。インフレ率は中銀目標の２％を上回って推移し、政
府の現金給付などの消費支援も効果が一巡したことから、個人消費は下振れた。成果給の恩
恵は一部の労働者にとどまり、業種間の賃金格差の拡大も懸念される。ソウルを中心に不動
産価格の上昇が続くなか、 家計債務を巡る金融リスクもくすぶっており、 中銀は10 月会合で
政策金利を据え置きつつも、追加利上げに含みを持たせた。KOSPI はピークから約４割下落
しており、金融市場の調整が家計に与える影響にも注意が必要である。 
○ 為替市場では、株価調整とは対照的に、ウォン相場が底堅く推移している。過去最高を更新
した貿易黒字に伴う実需と、中銀のタカ派姿勢が下支えとなっている。 
○ 政治面では、李在明政権の支持率が過去最低に近い水準にある。企業と家計、資産保有層と
それ以外の層との間で景気回復の恩恵に格差が生じる「Ｋ字型」の様相が強まっており、金
融市場の混乱が家計部門に波及すれば、政権の求心力が一段と低下する可能性がある。

【世界的なAI・半導体需要の旺盛さを追い風に、足元の韓国景気は堅調な推移が続いている】 
韓国では、 世界的なＡＩ （人工知能） ・ 半導体関連需要の旺盛な動きが景気の追い風となる展開が
続いている（注１）。韓国は、DRAM やNAND 型フラッシュメモリ、広帯域メモリ（ＨＢＭ）などメモ
リ半導体の大規模量産で世界トップクラスの競争力を有する。旺盛な半導体需要をけん引役に９月
の輸出額は過去最高額を更新するなど、輸出の勢いは強まっている。韓国はＧＤＰに占める輸出の

注１ 10 月１日付レポート「韓国､AI・半導体需要が景気に追い風も､政治への逆風は止まず」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/666170.html）

### Page 2

2/5 
比率が４割を上回るなど、 アジア太平洋
地域のなかでも外需依存度が相対的に
高い。こうしたなか、 ７-９月の実質ＧＤ
Ｐ成長率は前期比＋1.0％、年率換算ベ
ースでは＋3.93％となり、 景気の堅調さ
が確認されている （図１）。 中期的な基調
を示す前年同期比ベースの成長率も＋
3.4％と前期（同＋3.7％）から伸びが鈍
化しているものの、 ３四半期連続で３％
を上回る水準で推移している。 
世界的なＡＩ・半導体関連需要の拡大
を反映して財輸出が押し上げられるとともに（図２）、中国人観光客を中心とする来訪者数の増加
はサービス輸出を押し上げるなど、堅調な外需が景気をけん引する動きがみられる。さらに、 ＡＩ ・
半導体関連需要の旺盛さを追い風に、 半導体製造設備やデータセンター関連の投資が拡大するなど、
設備投資も景気を下支えしている。ま
た、政府消費の増加も成長に寄与した。
一方で、政府はイラン情勢の悪化を受け
た原油高の影響軽減を目的に、低所得者
層などを対象に１人当たり 10 万～60 万
ウォンの現金給付を実施したほか、 省エ
ネ家電の購入促進策 （キャッシュバック
の実施）などの消費支援に動いた。しか
し、足元ではその効果が一巡しており、
個人消費は下振れしている。さらに、前
期は在庫調整が進む動きが確認された
ものの、 当期は前期比年率ベースで＋0.32pt と試算されるなど、在庫の積み上がりを示唆する動き
もうかがえる。 
とはいえ、2026 年の９月までの累計ベースの経済成長率は＋3.6％となり、中銀が８月時点で示
した見通し （＋3.3％）を上回っている。 中銀は10 月会合において、2026 年の経済成長率が８月時
点の見通しを大きく上回る可能性を示唆したが、 足元の状況はそうした見方に沿った動きと捉えら
れる。 さらに、2027 年についても、 世界的なＡＩ・半導体関連需要の動向に留意する必要があるも
のの、外需の堅調さが景気をけん引する展開が続く可能性が見込まれる。

### Page 3

3/5 
【家計部門はインフレに直面、賃金上昇の恩恵は一部にとどまり、不動産高騰や株価調整も重し】 
外需は堅調な動きをみせる一方、対照的に個人消費は力強さを欠いている。背景には、2025 年後
半以降のインフレ率が中銀目標（２％）を上回る伸びで推移するなど、実質購買力に下押し圧力が
かかっていることがある。さらに、イラ
ン情勢の悪化を受けた 原油高を背景に
物価上昇圧力が残り、インフレ率は中銀
目標を上回る水準で推移している （図
３） 。これを受けて、政府は燃料価格の上
限規制 を約６ヵ月維持するなど物価対
策に動いている。しかし、足元では中東
情勢 の不透明 感を理由に 原油価格は高
止まりするなど、見通しが立たない状況
が続いている。一方で、半導体産業など
一部企業では、営業利益などの一定割合
（Ｎ％）を従業員の成果報酬の原資とする「Ｎ％成果給」が導入され、他産業にも導入を求める動
きが広がっている。 とはいえ、実際に高額な成果報酬の恩恵を受ける労働者は一部にとどまるなど、
業種間の賃金格差の拡大に繋がる懸念も高まっている。 
韓国ではここ数年、首都ソウルを中心とする不動産価格の高騰が社会問題化してきた。 政府は
2025 年以降に 様々な不動産高騰対策を
打ち出したものの、一連の対策では実需
層の資金調達が困難になる一方、規制対
象外の地域で不動産価格が上昇するな
ど「副作用」も顕在化した。また、政府
は超高額住宅や所有者が居住していな
い住宅に対する課税強化を目指したも
のの、中高所得者層などからの反発を受
けて一部見直しを余儀なくされた。さら
に、一連の政府主導による不動産対策に
対する不満は、６月に実施されたソウル
市長選で与党候補が敗北した一因との見方もある。とはいえ、 足元においてもソウルを中心とする
不動産価格は上昇が続いており （図４） 、住宅関連融資の増加が続くなど、 家計債務を巡る金融リス
クもくすぶっている。中銀は、10 月の定例会合で３会合ぶりに政策金利を据え置いたものの、 追加
利上げに含みを持たせる考えを示した（注２）。背景のひとつには、 不動産市場を巡るリスクがある

注２ 10 月22 日付レポート「韓国中銀、株価急落などで利上げ休止も、金融引き締めの長期化を示唆」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/666810.html）

### Page 4

4/5 
と考えられる。 
2025 年以降の韓国金融市場では、世界的なＡＩ・半導体関連需要の旺盛な動きを追い風に、主要
株価指数（KOSPI）は上昇基調を強めた。
さらに、 政府は2025 年末、 個人投資家が
保有する海外株式を売却して国内株式
に１年間投資した場合、一定の条件の下
で譲渡所得税を最大 100％控除する時限
措置を発表し、2026 年３月に法制化し
た。そのうえ、株価上昇を受けて個人投
資家の間でレバレッジ投資が活発化し
たことも、株価上昇の動きを後押しする
一助となった。しかし、構成銘柄全体の
時価総額に占める割合が５割前後を占
めるサムスン電子とＳＫハイニックスの２社の株価動向にKOSPI が左右されやすい構造にある。 こ
うしたなか、足元ではピークから約４割下落するなど、市場は大幅な調整に直面している（図５）
（注３）。こうした金融市場の不安定な動きも、家計部門に悪影響を与えている可能性がある。

【株価の大幅調整が続いているものの、貿易黒字と中銀のタカ派姿勢がウォン相場を下支え】 
金融市場においては、前述の通り半導体関連株を中心とする株価調整の動きに直面しているもの
の、 通貨ウォン相場は対照的に底堅い動きをみせている （図６） 。 背景には、世界的なＡＩ・半導体
関連需要の旺盛さが輸出を押し上げる
なか、足元の貿易黒字は過去最高を更新
するなど、ウォンに対する実需が生まれ
やすい環境にあることが挙げられる。ま
た、中銀が断続的な利上げを実施すると
ともに、先行きも追加利上げに含みを持
たせるなど「タカ派」姿勢を強めている
ことがある。さらに、 中銀の申総裁は10
月の定例会合後に行った記者会見にお
いて、「足元のウォンの対ドル相場は歴
史的に見れば低水準」 、 「実質実効ベース
でもウォンは依然割安」、「ウォン高は経済的不平等を是正する効果がある」と述べるなど、ウォン
高の経済的な利点を指摘したことも、ウォン相場を後押ししている可能性がある。

注３ 10 月15 日付レポート「韓国株が急落、AI・半導体好況の陰で市場の調整が深まる」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/666600.html）

### Page 5

経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。
5/5
【金融市場の混乱や家計部門を取り巻く環境が李在明政権の行方を左右する可能性に要注意】
こうしたなか、李在明（イ・ジェミョン）政権の支持率は低水準で推移しており、足元では過去
最低に近い水準で推移している。 このところの支持率低下を巡っては、 若年層などで支持が弱まる
動きがみられるほか、 不動産政策や物価高への不満も影響していると考えられる。 こうした動きは、
政権が支持層をつなぎとめることの難しさを示唆している。足元の景気はいわゆる「Ｋ字型」の様
相を強めるとともに、 企業と家計、 資産保有層とそれ以外の層との間で景気回復の恩恵に格差が生
じており、その格差が一段と拡大する兆しもみられる。このため、先行きは実体経済の堅調さにも

かかわらず、李政権を取り巻く環境が厳しさを増す可能性も考えられる。与党・共に民主党を巡っ
ても、党内では政策運営などを巡る意見対立がみられるなど、火種を抱えている。金融市場の混乱
が家計部門に波及することで、 李政権の求心力をさらに弱める可能性がある点には注意が必要であ
る。
以 上

## Document 333: Financial conditions in the Eurozone_ a tightening temporarily tempered by credit volumes (2026-10-07)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Financial conditions in the Eurozone_ a tightening temporarily tempered by credit volumes (2026-10-07).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-07
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

7 October 2026
The bank
for a changing
world
FINANCIAL CONDITIONS IN THE EUROZONE: A TIGHTENING TEMPORARILY TEMPERED BY CREDIT VOLUMES
Thomas Humblot
*LATEST OBSERVATION: 5 OCTOBER 2026  **ESTIMATE
Since June 2026, the ECB has been publishing 1 a new indicator that helps to assess the transmission of its monetary policy to the economy:
the Broad Intermediation Gauge 2 (ECB-BIG). This new tool has revealed that, thus far, strong credit growth has limited the restrictive impact

of tightening financial conditions caused by rising interest rates in the Eurozone. However, this offsetting effect is beginning to fade.
The ECB-BIG: a new indicator for a broader view of financial conditions
The ECB-BIG complements the range of indicators used by the European Central Bank (ECB) to assess the effective transmission of its monetary
policy. This new tool provides a comprehensive view of how restrictive (or expansive) financial conditions are in the Eurozone. It stands out

for its approach that focuses more specifically on intermediation conditions. Therefore, the ECB-BIG provides a more comprehensive view than
most other financial condition indicators of financial conditions. In fact, it covers variables relating to the volume of bank lending to households
and businesses (the ‘volume’ component). Traditionally, this type of indicator tends to focus more on market variables (primarily interest and
exchange rates), share prices and corporate spreads. In addition, the ‘price’ component of the ECB-BIG is extended to include the cost of bank

loans, which is another new feature.
The rise in interest rates was temporarily offset by increased lending
By aggregating the variables relating to banking and non-banking intermediation conditions, the ECB-BIG shows that rising interest rates
(negative price effect) were offset in particular by lending volumes, which have continued to rise in 2026 (positive volume effect). A more
‘traditional’ indicator of financial conditions, the Macro-Finance FCI (MF-FCI) — which is, incidentally, included in the ECB-BIG — reveals a

much more pronounced tightening over the same period (see the orange curve in the chart). However, this offsetting of the price effect by the
volume effect is now beginning to wane, a sign that monetary policy is being effectively transmitted to the economy. Financial intermediaries
are gradually passing on the rise in rates to their customers (see the dotted green lines, which represent the composite indicators of the cost
of new loans to households and businesses, combining interest rates for different maturities), while lending volumes are falling. As a result,

between July and August 2026, seasonally adjusted flows of loans to households and businesses almost halved.
1 P . Lane, 2026, The Outlook for the Euro Area Economy, Background slides for Reuters NEXT Europe, London, 16 June 2026
2 A. Allayioti et al., 2026, The ECB-BIG index: tracking credit conditions in the euro area, ECB Economic Bulletin, Issue 5/2026
SOURCE: ALLAYIOTI AND AL. (2026), ECB, BLETZINGER ET AL. (2026), BNP PARIBAS ESTIMATES

In the longer term, financial conditions are expected to tighten, including as measured by the ECB-BIG
Despite the very sharp widening of sovereign spreads between 28 September and 2 October, financial conditions as measured by the
MF-FCI did not tighten, with sovereign yields actually falling over this period (with the exception of the OAT and the BTP), for example.

Nevertheless, the high volatility of the bond market, particularly in the sovereign segment, is likely to continue to affect financial
conditions. Looking slightly further ahead, the ECB’s key interest rate rises — the one that occurred on 16 September and another that we
anticipate for December 2026 (both +25 bp) — are likely to result in a more pronounced tightening. With a time lag of a number of weeks,
financial conditions, as defined by the ECB-BIG, are also expected to tighten. The price effect is likely to lead to a negative volume effect

on this occasion.

## Document 391: Q3 Growth in advanced economies_ solid despite shocks (2026-10-23)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Q3 Growth in advanced economies_ solid despite shocks (2026-10-23).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-23
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Paris, 23 October 2026

EcoFlash

Q3 Growth in advanced economies: solid despite shocks

Growth looks to have held up in Q3 despite successive shocks (see our analysis), as it did in 2025 and H1 2026. A longer 
Middle East conflict has fed into more persistent inflation; drought has worsened in many countries, and interest rates 
have risen further – at the short end, with both the ECB and the Fed raising policy rates in September, and even more at 
the long end. However, our nowcasts show that growth is bending without breaking in most advanced economies. GDP 
figures (due on 29 October in the US and 30 October for the euro area and its member states) are expected to confirm 
that. In the Euro area, momentum looks stable (excluding Ireland, whose GDP is volatile). In Germany, growth has been 
uninterrupted since Q4 2025, while France is likely to have returned to growth after stagnating in H1. In the US, growth 
is likely to have gathered pace, according to the GDP Now, still driven by AI, but increasingly by other sectors as well.

Euro area: resilience beneath the surface. Our nowcast points to growth slowing to 0.3% in q/q, though the pace is stable 
excluding Ireland (Irish GDP is volatile and had markedly boosted the Q2 figure). Excluding Ireland, growth should hold at its H2 
average, a sign of the bloc’s underlying resilience. Also, momentum has broadened to a growing number of sectors ( see our 
analysis). While industrial production stalled over the summer, services remained buoyant. The services PMI rebounded sharply 
in Q3 and sits firmly in expansion territory (53.0 in September). Improving job-related survey data (PMI and European Commission 
surveys) and a stable unemployment rate (6.4% in August) hint at more hiring in Q3 – a support for household consumption that 
partly offsets purchasing-power losses from persistent inflation. Corporate investment is underpinned by buoyant new investment 
lending (8.7% y/y In August, 12-month cumulative). 
France: growing, nevertheless. The succession of shocks does not appear to have prevented France from returning to 
growth in Q3, at 0.2% q/q, according to our nowcast. One-off factors accounted for the stall in activity in H1. In Q3, the drought 
worsened and industrial production remained subdued. However, market services production rebounded in July and August, 
notably in business services, restaurants, digital and real estate. The latter points to residential investment recovering after a weak 
H1. Corporate investment likely followed, as reflected in buoyant credit flows and AI-related investment. Household consumption 
is likely to have improved slightly on higher electric vehicle sales.  
Euro area  France

Source: Refinitiv, BNP Paribas  Source: Refinitiv, BNP Paribas

Germany: growth still solid. The H1 rebound in activity is moderating but continues. Our nowcast points to a fourth straight 
quarter of growth in Q3, at 0.2% q/q (after 0.3% in Q4 2025, 0.4% in Q1 2026, and 0.3% in Q2). Manufacturing activity eased 
slightly, according to the September PMI survey (53.9, down 0.4 m/m). Government spending will remain growth-supportive, with 
defence and infrastructure outlays gaining traction. Infrastructure spending continues to underpin activity in public works, which 
stands at its highest since November 2021 (2.5% 3m/3m in August). Exports (4.4% y/y between January and August) remain the 
main growth engine, helped by a pivot towards Europe (7.2%). 
United States: an expansion undeterred by inflation. Growth is likely to have gathered pace in Q3. The Atlanta Fed’s GDP 
Now points to a 3.5% annualized growth rate (AR), close to our own 3.7% forecast (0.9% non -AR) and up from 2.2% in 
Q2. Domestic demand remains the engine. Consumption is weathering the pickup in inflation, while the AI-driven capex cycle 
remains robust – as reflected in retail sales and nondefence capital-goods orders (excl. aircraft). As a result, growth in final sales

### Page 2

to private domestic purchasers 1 should print above 4% AR again. By contrast, external trade should remain a drag on growth, 
reflecting AI-related import needs. Survey data point to mome ntum that is both solid and broad -based. The ISM indices have 
remained firmly in growth territory (54.9 quarterly average for manufacturing and services alike). At the same time, they highlight 
persistent price and supply-chain pressures.

Germany   United States

Source: Refinitiv, BNP Paribas   Source: Refinitiv, Atlanta Fed, BNP Paribas

are based are reasonable, the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
August inflation came in soft...
Inflation moderated in August following July’s strong 
outcome. The CPI rose 0.4% in the month, in line with our 
expectation but slightly below market consensus of 0.5%, 
taking the annual rate to 4.0%. On a seasonally adjusted 
basis, the CPI increased 0.7%mth, underscoring the impact of 
seasonal discounting in August and the start of the financial 
year repricing in July.
More closely watched are the measures of underlying inflation 
including the Trimmed Mean (TM) and market services. 
The TM rose 0.2% in August, in line with our expectations 
but again softer than the market, leaving the annual pace 
unchanged at 3.6% for a third consecutive month. Meanwhile, 
market services excluding volatiles & holiday travel increased 
0.2%mth/4.1%yr. Notable strength (three-month annualised, 
seasonally adjusted) was seen in sports participation (10.9%), 
clothing repair (7.0%), restaurant meals (6.8%) and other 
household services (5.2%). For full analysis of the August CPI, 
see “August CPI – Came in on our expectations ”.
The expenditure-weighted share of the CPI basket 
experiencing positive price shocks eased to 11% in August 
from 15% in July, while the share experiencing negative price 
shocks declined from 17% to just below 13%. The majority of 
the positive shocks were concentrated in consumer durables 
On a three-month moving average basis, the net momentum 
share (positive minus negative) rose to –4.5% from –7.7% 
in July and a trough of –15.8% in May, suggesting the 
disinflationary impulse may be nearing exhaustion. 
... with September due for a moderate pick-up
Looking ahead to September, early indicators painted a 
mixed picture. The NAB business survey showed retail price 
growth slowing to 0.9%qtr from 1.1%qtr in August, with 
labour cost growth easing to 1.7%qtr from 1.8%qtr, while 
purchase cost growth edged up to 2.4%qtr from 2.3%qtr. 
Nevertheless, all measures remain well above their long-run 
averages while the gap between purchase costs and final 
prices continues to widen, indicating businesses are facing 
margin compression (see here for further detail). Meanwhile, 
consumers’ year-ahead inflation expectations held steady 
at 4.9% in September (easing slightly at 2dp) but surged to 
5.3% in October likely reflecting higher fuel prices, with retail 
petrol and diesel averaging $2.20/l (+8.2%mth) and $2.68/l 
(+7.7%mth). We do believe the auto fuel prices are likely to see 
a near term peak in October.  Meanwhile, global shipping costs 
have also surged recently amid renewed Middle East tensions.
Sep quarter unrevised but Dec upgraded significantly
Justin Smirk, Senior Economist
Neha Sharma, Economist

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
The September CPI is expected to rise 0.6% (0.62% to 2dp)
with the annual pace lifting to 4.1%yr. Our seasonally adjusted
estimate increases 0.5% (0.48% to 2dp). For the monthly
TM, we expect a 0.3% (0.26% to 2dp) lift, holding the annual

pace steady at 3.6%. On a 3m/3m basis, the TM is tracking
a 1.0% gain in September (noting that while this measure is
conceptually different to the quarterly TM it still serves as a
useful guide). For market services excluding volatiles & holiday
travel we have pencilled in another 0.2% rise, slowing the
annual pace slightly to 4.0% (a nine-month low).
The largest contributors are recreation & culture (+0.27ppt),
driven wholly by holiday travel & accommodation. Transport

(+0.23ppt) makes a solid contribution, with gains in fuel
(+0.26ppt) partly offset by motor vehicles (–0.02ppt). Housing
also contributes (+0.13ppt), with rents and new dwellings
each adding 0.02ppt while property rates add 0.08ppt.
The key detractors in the month are food & non-alcoholic
beverages (–0.08ppt) and garments (–0.04ppt).
Key details for the month of September
Food & non-alcoholic beverages are expected to fall
0.5%mth, easing the annual pace to 2.8%yr. The weakness is

concentrated in a 3.7%mth decline in fruit & vegetables, while
non-alcoholic beverages are also expected to fall 1.5%mth.
Meals out & takeaway is forecast to rise 0.1%mth, easing the
annual rate to 3.9%.
Housing is expected to rise 0.6% in September, slowing the
annual pace to 5.5%yr. Component-wise:
• Rents are expected to rise 0.3%mth and 3.7%yr. CPI rent
inflation is nearing its peak, consistent with the moderation
in asking rent growth. We expect rent inflation to ease to

3.6%yr by end-2026 and 2.5%yr by end-2027.
• New dwelling prices, which capture only the building
component of construction costs, are expected to rise
0.2%mth and 4.9%yr. Ongoing weakness in established
housing markets would be increasing builders’ difficulty in
passing through higher costs to new builds. As a result, we
expect new dwelling inflation to moderate to 4.5%yr by
end-2026 and 2.1%yr by end-2027.
Transport inflation is expected to rise 2.0%mth in September,

lifting the annual pace to 7.7%yr reflecting an 8.3%mth
increase in fuel prices following higher oil prices through the
month. Motor vehicle prices and spare parts are expected to
fall 0.7%mth and 1.3%mth, respectively. In NSW, a one-year
vehicle registration discount between $80-100 came into
effect from 1 September, which is likely to weigh on the other
services in respect of motor vehicles component.
Recreation & culture is expected to rise 2.1%mth driven by a

4.4%mth increase in holiday travel, partly offset by a 0.4%
decline in audio, visual & computing equipment & services and
a 1.3% fall in sporting services.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
Risks to September baseline
We view the risks around the September CPI outcome as broadly 
balanced. Waning spending momentum outside of fuel may make 
it difficult for businesses to pass through higher costs in the near 
term. However, some of these pricing pressures may be deferred 
rather than forgone. The October ban on card surcharging may 
prompt a greater-than-usual degree of price resetting, allowing 
businesses to recover some of the margin pressures that have built 
up in recent months.
Risks to housing are mixed. Trade materials suppliers continued 
to announce above-average price increases through September 
(around 8%). While soft conditions in the established housing 
market may limit the extent to which these costs are passed 
through to new dwelling prices, they nevertheless present an 
upside risk to housing-related inflation, particularly maintenance 
& repair and insurance costs. Growth in advertised rents also 
continues to ease but these new leases take some time to flow 
through to average rents paid measured in the CPI. 
A kick up in October ...
We have a solid preliminary estimate for the October CPI of 
0.6%mth lifting the annual pace to 4.8%yr. It is true that the key 
driver is an 8.9% increase in auto fuel but we estimate the TM to 
lift 0.4%mth/3.7%yr highlighting broader inflationary pressures. 
We expect that the removal of credit charge surcharges and 
resulting increase in retail prices, at most, will contribute 0.1ppt 
but only just so see it as more of a rounding error. Nevertheless, 
these changes come at a time of a significant upstream price 
pressure, particularly for SMEs, so there is some risk of broader 
price adjustments. As such, we expect marginally larger than 
usual price adjustments in October particularly in hospitality 
and household services. Being the first month of the quarter the 
October lift sets a higher base for the quarterly average estimates 
resulting in an upgrade to our December quarter estimate.
We have included a table with a monthly profile for the CPI, 
seasonally adjusted CPI and the TM out to June 2027. At 
June 2027 we have an annual pace of 3.7%yr for the CPI and 
seasonally adjusted CPI and 3.3%yr for the TM. There is also a 
table breaking down the monthly components of the CPI through 
the December quarter. 
... highlights stronger inflationary pressure ...
Our September quarter CPI estimate is unrevised at 1.3%qtr 
with the annual pace steady at 3.9%yr. At the margin there 
was a 0.04ppt increase due to a lift in auto fuel but this is 
1.29%qtr at two decimal places so still rounds to 1.3%. 
The seasonally adjusted CPI gains 1.1% in the September 
quarter highlighting that part of the increase is due to usual 
seasonality. Our TM estimate for Q3 has a small downward 
revision of 0.03ppt to 0.88% at two decimal place so our 
published estimate is also unrevised at 0.9%qtr. 
... resulting in an upward revision to Q4 2026
Westpac’s December quarter CPI estimate has been boosted 
from a 0.5%qtr to 1.3%qtr. Driving most of this change from 
our previous estimate is the 15.3% increase in autofuel, it

was 1.9%, contributing 0.55ppt to the 0.8ppt increase in the 
quarterly estimate. Outside of a 0.09ppt contribution to the 
revision from holiday travel & accommodation (revised up from 
4.2% to 5.6%) all else was via small contributions for a large 
number of expenditure categories. This is why there is a 0.2ppt 
increase in our September quarter TM estimate from 0.7%qtr 
to 0.9%qtr.
A disinflationary impulse kicks in for 2027
We now expect auto fuel prices to peak in October (240 cpl 
for petrol, 284 cpl for diesel) and then to trend lower through 
2027 (184 cpl for petrol and 194 cpl for diesel by December 
2027) leading to a 13.6% falling in auto fuel in the first half 
of 2027 subtracting 0.46ppt off the CPI over the same period 
of time. For a detailed description on how we constructed the 
auto fuels profile please see: “ Persistent energy pressures 
have further to run ” 
The first half of 2027 will also see an almost 5% decline in 
holiday travel & accommodation which will subtract a further 
0.29ppt off the CPI. However, outside of that any broader 
moderation in inflation is expected to be more muted hence 
we have the TM printing 0.7%qtr for each quarter in 2027.  
Critical to this moderation in core inflation is our expect step 
down in dwelling price inflation on the back of falling estab -
lished home prices. From a 1.1%qtr increase in Q3 2026 we 
see dwelling price increasing by 0.7% in the Q4 2026, and 
then 0.6% in both Q1 and Q2 2027 before dropping to 0.5%qtr 
in Q3 and Q4 2027. This will see dwelling price inflation in the 
CPI drop from 5.4%yr at June 2026 to 3.0%yr by June 2027 
then down to 2.2%yr by December 2027. 
At least for the near term a key uncertainty remains the extent 
to which businesses are able to pass through ongoing labour 
and non-labour cost pressures and how this unwinds through 
2027 as energy prices ease and the domestic economy slows 
under the weight of higher interest rates and falling estab -
lished home prices. There is a risk that firms face greater 
resistance to price increases if discretionary household spend -
ing softens by more than expected. That said, these headwinds 
may be at least partially offset by the stronger starting point 
for household incomes, which has proven more resilient than 
previously anticipated, and by higher energy prices due to the 
ongoing conflict in the Middle East.
That said, there is also the risk that the disinflationary pulse by 
the second half of 2027 could be greater than what we antici-

and thus a more significant moderation in price setting behav -
iour. It is also possible that our auto fuel prices fall by more 
than we anticipate. For while we have spot crude prices to fall 
to about pre-crisis levels by end 2027, a combination of a slow 
recovery in refining capacity, constrained return of physical 
supplies and higher shipping cost keep Australian fuel prices 
above their pre-conflict level. Should all these factors improve 
fast than we expect, and auto fuel prices return to pre-conflict 
level if not lower, then headline inflation could dip below 2%yr 
and the TM would be closer to 2.5%yr (the mid point if the 
RBA’s inflation target), by end 2027.

## Document 417: Top of Mind - Assessing a Less Transparent Fed

### Page 1

The Goldman Sachs Group, Inc.
Fed Chairman Kevin Warsh is steering the Fed into a less transparent era. So, is that
a positive or negative development, and what could it mean for markets and the
economy? We speak with Former Fed governors Donald Kohn and Stephen Miran
as well as GS’ Jan Hatzius who agree that rigid “Odyssean” forward guidance that
commits policymakers to a pre-set path risks policy mistakes and is therefore rarely
appropriate. But they somewhat disagree about the benefits of great er transparency

around the Fed’s reaction function. Hatzius and Kohn argue that such transparency
helps markets better anticipate and price in policy changes, accelerating monetary
policy transmission. But Miran believes that the line between committing to a path
and being transparent is clearer in theory than in practice. And while GS economists
and strategists find that reduc ed transparency leads to increased market volatility,
the question is whether this just increases the market’s noise or also improves the

market’s signal. Hatzius argues largely for the former and Miran for the latter.
Jan Hatzius, Chief Economist and Head of Global Investment Research, Goldman Sachs
“Markets price what they think the Fed will do, not what they think the Fed should do. That will remain true even if the Fed
obscures its reaction function… market guesses about the Fed’s next moves will simply become worse, leading to more
volatility... and importantly, volatility that serves no constructive economic purpose.”

Donald Kohn, former Vice Chairman of the Board of Governors of the Federal Reserve System, Robert V . Roosa
Chair in International Economics and Senior Fellow, Brookings Institution
“I sympathize with Chairman Warsh to some extent. [But] the real problem with Warsh’s approach is his reluctance to articulate
how the FOMC sees the economy evolving… explaining the Fed’s thinking about the economic outlook is essential in helping
markets act as a stabilizing force and is a core component of accountability.”
